\begin{abstract}Every move below was produced by relational analogy to named master games (human and/or top-engine -- the exact source is named per move). Each figure shows our position with Melanie's move (a) and the top retrieved analog positions (b--d) with the master move played there; the caption names the exact source and the piece-role correspondence under $\mu$ -- cross-type maps (a rook playing a queen's role) are called out, as no material/eval engine explains a move that way.\end{abstract}

\caption{\textbf{Move 7, Black: \texttt{Nb6}.} (a) Our position; Melanie plays \texttt{Nb6} (\texttt{d5b6}). Panels (b)--(d) are the retrieved master positions whose correspondence most drove this move (highest QAP objective among those that transfer to it). (b) Retrieved from \texttt{ccrl\_top\_p0000\#2888} (ply 9) [structural distance $d$=43, QAP obj=73]: the master played \texttt{Nb6}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Nd4} plays the role of that game's \emph{Nd4}; cross-type roles: our \emph{R}a8 as their \emph{B}c8, our \emph{n}f3 as their \emph{R}h8; transferred move \texttt{d5b6} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. (c) Retrieved from \texttt{ccrl\_top\_p0000\#2889} (ply 9) [structural distance $d$=43, QAP obj=73]: the master played \texttt{Nb6}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Nd4} plays the role of that game's \emph{Nd4}; cross-type roles: our \emph{R}a8 as their \emph{B}c8, our \emph{n}f3 as their \emph{R}h8; transferred move \texttt{d5b6} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. (d) Retrieved from \texttt{ccrl\_top\_p0000\#534} (ply 13) [structural distance $d$=38, QAP obj=70]: the master played \texttt{Nb6}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Nd4} plays the role of that game's \emph{Nd4}; cross-type roles: our \emph{B}c8 as their \emph{R}a8, our \emph{p}f2 as their \emph{B}c8; transferred move \texttt{d5b6} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. Synthesis: 9 retrieved analogs transferred to \texttt{Nb6} (summed QAP objective 636, the strongest correspondence among the candidate moves); Melanie committed it via the \texttt{search} selector.}
